# Nonmatching functions

Every function that is in C but doesn't compile to the original. The addresses are Black 2's and White 2's.
`tools/decomp/compiler_probe.py FILE --version b2_us` (and `w2_us`) shows the difference of each function in a file,
and `--show-diff 1.1p1` the instructions.

## In C, not matching

These functions are written as the closest natural C found. Their files are partial (no `complete` in `delinks.txt`), so
the original code is linked until they match. The differences are the same in both versions.

| File | Function | Address | Difference |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `src/ov036/shortcut_menu.c` | `func_ov036_021bedd8` | `0x021bedd8` / `0x021bee10` | 8 bytes short (0x224 against 0x22c). Open. |
| `src/ov036/shortcut_menu.c` | `SetupMenu` | `0x021bf204` / `0x021bf24c` | 2 bytes short (0x82 against 0x84). The original sets `sys_memset_fast`, keeps the window in a register across the three flush calls and reloads it from `work->window` for `printWindow.window`; ours keeps it in `r4`. Tried: no local window, the local declared before and after the member stores, `printWindow` filled before the flushes, and the arguments of `BmpWin_CreateDynamic` written as literals and reordered. Open. |
| `src/ov036/shortcut_menu.c` | `ReadInput` | `0x021bf3a8` / `0x021bf450` | 0xc short (0x1b0 against 0x1bc). The original's frame is 0x14 with `count` on the stack at `sp` and the six `u16` locals at `sp+4` to `sp+0xe`; ours is 0x10 with `count` in `r7` and the locals at `sp` to `sp+0xa`, so every access is offset by 4 and one register more is pushed. Tried: all 504 orders of the locals with the six `u16` in order, `volatile count`, `count` as `u32`/`u16`/`s16`, `handled` as `u8`/`u32` with and without an initializer, `newIndex` inlined and as `u8`/`s32`. Open. |
| `src/ov036/shortcut_menu.c` | `RedrawHighlight` | `0x021bf5e8` / `0x021bf690` | 6 bytes larger (0x10a against 0x104). The original reuses `r5` for the colour 0xc across the first `GFL_BGSysSetScrPaletteNo` and parks `rowHeight` in `r6`; ours rematerializes 0xc in `r0` and spills `rowHeight` into a high register. Tried: the colour as a `u8` local, the multiply before the add, `rowH` removed and as `s32`, a `diff` local, and the parameters inlined. Open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `prepareForPokemonNameEntryData` | `0x021bf914` / `0x021bf94c` | Same size, 30 bytes. The order of the getTrainerGameInfoAddress chain is the one the original uses; the rest of the difference is open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `PokeNameEntry_Callback` | `0x021bf9d4` / `0x021bfa0c` | 8 bytes short (0x8c against 0x94). Open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `JoinAvenueNameEntry_Create` | `0x021bfa68` / `0x021bfaa0` | 0x1c short (0x104 against 0x120). Open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `JoinAvenueNameEntry_Callback` | `0x021bfb88` / `0x021bfbc0` | 0x18 short (0xfc against 0x114). Open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `RivalNameEntry_Callback` | `0x021bfcf4` / `0x021bfd2c` | 0xc short (0x70 against 0x7c). Open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `EventPokeNameWordSetInput_Create` | `0x021bfd70` / `0x021bfda8` | Same size, 1 byte: the original loads r1 with 0x10 before the store of the result, ours with 0. Moving the work struct's assignments and the declarations of the locals did not move it. Open. |
| `src/ov036/event_name_entry.c` | `EventPokeNameWordSetInput_Callback` | `0x021bfdd8` / `0x021bfe10` | 4 bytes short (0xac against 0xb0). Open. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_InitGraphics` | `0x0219a16c` / `0x0219a1ac` | Same size, 8 bytes: in three calls (`GFL_G3DSysSetSwapBufferParams(0, 0)` after `G3X_EdgeMarking`, `GFL_BGSysSetBGEnabled(0, TRUE)`, `gfxRegSetAlphaBlend(REG_DB_BLDCNT_ADDR, 0, 0xf, 4, 16)`) the original passes the zero from `r7` (`adds r0, r7, #0`) where ours loads `movs r0, #0`; the other zero arguments of the function are literal in both. Tried FALSE for the arguments; the prototypes are `u32`/`u8`. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_InitItems` | `0x0219a6c0` / `0x0219a700` | Same size, 6 bytes: two stack slots of the texture loop swap (the original keeps `dimPlttKey` at `sp+0x14` and `size` at `sp+0x18`, ours the reverse). Tried several declaration orders of the loop's locals (the best of them is kept) and a 120-variant permutation; the carousel loop's counter is `k`, an int, which the original has (`blt`), and the badge actor kept in a local `actor` matched the size. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_SortEntries` | `0x0219c7e8` / `0x0219c828` | Same size, 18 bytes: stack slots only. The original has `n` at `sp+8`, the `work + first * 4` temporary at `sp+0xc`, `best` at `sp+0x10` and `first` at `sp+0x14`; ours puts `n` at `sp+0xc` and the temporary at `sp+8`. `category` is `u8` as `GetSortKey`'s parameter (that removed the narrowing) and `n` a `u16`. Tried function-level and block-level declarations and every order of `first`, `best`, `n`. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_UpdateReturning` | `0x0219c42c` / `0x0219c46c` | 8 bytes short, 95 lines: the original re-narrows the decremented frame count before `t = 10 - frames` (`lsls`/`lsrs` into `r1`), ours uses the register as is; its frame is 12 bytes larger (`sp - 0x7c`; `t`, `ox`, `oy` of the two loops separate). Tried `frames = frames - 1`, `--frames`, casts, `t`/`ox`/`oy` at function scope or per block. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_UpdateCarousel` | `0x0219ae1c` / `0x0219ae5c` | 0x38c against 0x3bc. The original builds its angle constants at run time (`mov r0, #2; lsls r0, r0, #0xe; lsrs r0, r0, #1` for 0x4000, `lsrs r5, r0, #3` for FX32_ONE, `(0x8000 - a)` then `+ 0x14000` as two steps) where MWCC folds them in ours, and keeps `(0x6e00 - a)` as a u16 before `<< 11` (kept with a cast); `idx` is a `u16` (kept). Casts of constants and `>>` forms all fold. Also the shared tail of the three scale branches is `SetScale` once after the branches in the original. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_SelectCategory` | `0x0219c0f0` / `0x0219c130` | Needs a header change to match (see HasWornInCategory); with it, 4 bytes short: the original reloads `work->category` for `PrintCategory` (`adds r1, #0xa4; ldr r1, [r1]`) where ours reuses `r4`, and sets up `mov r0, r5` before `movs r4, #8; movs r1, #8` in the equal-category branch. The `GetRingIndex` inline (same as Create's) and the duplicated `SortEntries` call per branch matched the rest. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_WearItem` | `0x0219bc04` / `0x0219bc44` | Same size once the header change below is made, 18 bytes: the original keeps `0x1000` in `r7` and the loop's `node` in `r6`, `z` in `r4`, loaded from the literal pool (`ldr r4, =0`, which the comma form `for (node = ..., z = 0; ...)` reproduces); ours has `0x1000` in `r6`, `node` in `r4`, `z` in `r7`. Declaration orders of `node`, `z`, `heldDraw`, and the loop's locals did not move them. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_StartReturn` | `0x0219baa8` / `0x0219bae8` | Same size, 2 bytes: the two `ldrh` of the item-id comparison are in the other order (the original loads the node's id into `r1` first, ours loads `entry->itemId`); both spellings of the comparison and an `if/else` form give the same code. The declaration of `vec` after the first statements is what matched the rest. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_FindNearestEquipPos` | `0x0219c374` / `0x0219c3b4` | 4 to 8 bytes short: the original has `i` as an int (`adds r5, r5, #1`, `blt`) with a loop-entry test (`cmp r5, #9; bge`) and passes it to `MusItemDraw_CanEquipPos` and `MusPokeDraw_GetEquipPos` without narrowing, while ours narrows for the `u8` parameter (`CanEquipPos` is `u8 pos` in the header; with the parameter as int the narrowing goes and the function is 8 bytes short). Also `x` goes through a `mov r0, r0` in the original. An `s32 i` declared first, `while` and `for (; ...)` forms did not bring the entry test. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_RotateHeld` | `0x0219cd6c` / `0x0219cdac` | 4 bytes short: in the original `nodePos->y` is loaded into `r6` before the first `FX32_CONST` conversion and `touchX - nodePos->x` is evaluated first; ours evaluates `touchY` first. Tried locals `dx`/`dy` (s16, s32, in both orders), `x`/`y` fx32 locals, swapped arguments. |
| `src/ov208/dup_fitting.c` | `DupFitting_Create` | `0x021998c0` / `0x02199900` | Same size, 15 bytes: the original loads `carouselPos` and starts `GetRingIndex`'s sum before the `sndCooldown = 10` store (`ldr r1, [r5, r0]` then `strh r6, [r5, r4]`); ours stores first. The ring-index inline (`if (pos >= total) return (pos - total) / STEP; return pos / STEP;`) is what matched the rest. A local `index` before the store makes it worse. |
| `src/ov260/wifibattlematch_net_fields.c` | `WifiBattleMatchNet_GetArenaFieldName` | `0x021bc070` / `0x021bc0b0` | Every function matches, but the file's `.data` (the ARENA field-name strings of the tables from 0x021c00e4) comes out in another order than the ROM's. The strings' declaration order still needs searching. |

Tried other types, `mode` stored first and the branches in other orders. |

| `src/ov260/wifibattlematch_link_random.c` | `WifiBattleMatchLink_TaskRatingSearch` | `0x021b38a0` / `0x021b38e0` | 4 bytes, same size: setting up `sys_memcpy(received, otherPlayer, size + 0xd4)`, the original adds 0xd4 before `mov r1, r7`, ours after. | `src/ov260/wifibattlematch_link_competition.c` | `WifiBattleMatchLink_CompetitionTaskMatch` | `0x021b6ca4` / `0x021b6ce4` | 743 bytes, same size: the original merges the error paths' `SetFunc` and `ResetCancel` tail into case 1's copy, ours into the last copy, and the rest shifts. | `src/ov260/wifibattlematch_net.c` | `WifiBattleMatchNet_CheckError` | `0x021b8e08` / `0x021b8e48` | 8 bytes short (0x138 against 0x140): the original keeps a dead `cmp r4, #0; bne` before the local-error half and gives the DWC half its own `mov r0, r4; pop`; ours folds both. The file's `.data` also differs: the equal-size strings `cnt`/`cup`/`mod`/`rat`/`deb`/`rul` and `RECORD_DATA_01` to `_06` come out in another order, and no declaration order of the file's objects gives the ROM's. |

| `src/ov117/gimmick_plasma_frigate.c` | `GimmickPlasmaFrigate_Init` | `0x021eef94` / `0x021eefd4` | The code matches, but the module check fails on `.rodata`: the two local `u16 rotations[4][3]` initializers of the panel and indicator loops (the same bytes) come out swapped (the ROM has the panel's at 0x021efd5c and the indicator's at 0x021efd74). Hoisting the tables to function scope fixes the order but changes the code (0x294 against 0x2a0); an inline helper for the indicator loop gives the layout but moves the stack slots; moving each static table to other positions flips them only while breaking the rest of the layout. Every function of the file matches. |

| `src/ov031/irc_link.c` | `IrcLink_SendFrame` | `0x02176018` / `0x02176058` | 4 bytes long (0x60 against 0x5c): both loops (the copy and the 0xaa xor) get a `cmp; ble` guard where the original jumps to the test at the bottom, and its zero stores share the register of `i`. Also tried: an `s16` accessor inline, an accumulate inline, `u32 sum`, and loops, which aren't unrolled. decomp-permuter found no better score in about 5,300 iterations. |

## Matching, but not linkable as is

- `src/ov266/staff_roll_main.c`'s `.rodata` comes out in another order than the original's, though the code that copies each BG setup matches. Ours lays out the proc table, the LCD setup, the line colors, sequence and command tables and the VRAM setup, then the BG setups 4, 0, 1, 3 and 5; the ROM has the proc table, the colors to the VRAM setup, the LCD setup, then the BG setups 0, 1, 5, 4 and 3. `rodata_order.py` and a search over every placement of the file-scope tables, with one extra object, found no declaration order that gives the ROM's layout; data that the original link dead-stripped may explain it, unconfirmed.

- `src/ov207/p_sta_sub.c`'s functions all match, but its `.rodata` can't be completed yet. It starts with an 8-byte
  object at `0x021bafc0` / `0x021bb000` (`7f 00 00 18 00 90 01 00`) that nothing references: a `static const` of it
  is dropped, while a global (`const u8 PStaSub_Unused[8]`) keeps a section of its own. With it, `rodata_order.py`'s
  model predicts the linked layout exactly when the named tables are listed before the 21 local initializers, which
  come in the order MWCC numbers them (`@4551` and so on). Yet no position or order of the three named tables gives
  the original's: in it, the initializers themselves come in another order, which the matching functions fix.

- `BattleHandler_AbilityPopupAdd` (`src/ov167/btl_server_flow.c`) failed to link when it was tried alone, before
  overlay 167 was split into its files, giving a second ARM/Thumb cross-overlay thunk at `0x021ac84c`, and matches
  now. Check it when its file is completed.

- `lib/dsprot/src/dsprot_stub_check_multi.c` and `dsprot_decrypt_multi.c` (overlay 337) match every function, but
  linked complete they fail the module check: the literal pool of `dsprot_cipher_multi.c`'s still-delinked code at
  `0x02180270` / `0x021802b0`, words of a code address plus 0x2200, then resolves to 0x2200 plus small offsets. They
  stay incomplete until those address-plus-0x2200 references link.

- `src/ov094/gym_mistralton.c`'s `.rodata` is the right size (0x15c4) but 32 bytes differ: equal-size 4- and 16-byte
  objects tie in another order than the original's, and no declaration order found (by `rodata_order.py`, over the
  animation lists, the local initializers and the 4-byte `sEndFrames` at `0x021f0234` / `0x021f0274`) gives it.
- `src/ov293/demo3d_data.c`'s 13 functions match, and its `.rodata` has the right size with every object of 16
  bytes or more in the ROM's place except two swapped pairs of 0x28-byte file tables, but its 389 tables of 2 to 10
  bytes are in another order. Per demo the ROM has set-up, commands, end commands, then file lists and tables, then
  units, with the scene table last; the order inside each demo's file lists is not found. The order within one size
  class depends only on where the objects of that size or larger are declared, so it can be solved class by class
  from the largest down.

## Keeping this list current

After changing a source file, run `compiler_probe.py` on it for both versions, and add, update or remove its rows. A file
gets `complete` in both versions' `delinks.txt` only when all its functions match and its data is written. The report at
`build/b2_us/report.json` helps find candidates, but its fuzzy match can be below 100 for a matching function because of
relocations, and misses some differences, so the probe decides.
